Bollinger breakout vs reversal: US100 H1

1 January 2020 → 31 December 2023 inclusive. Exness USTEC CFD, USD10,000 flat start, 1% equity target risk per position, broker-valid lots rounded down, 150 ms simulated execution delay.

The clip did not provide its code, instrument, exact bands, stop or session. This is a transparent baseline recreation, NOT a claim of reproducing its +72R, +122R or random-strategy significance. Historical 2020–2023 ticks are generated from minute bars, not authentic tick records. Intrabar fills and stop/target ordering are model-dependent. No post-2023/OOS test or 100-random-strategy comparison has been performed.

Frozen rules and assumptions

Bollinger 20 SMA ±2 population standard deviations of completed H1 closes, ATR14, initial stop 2×signal ATR. Indicators use the continuous CFD session; entries are restricted to the US cash session, weekdays 09:30–15:58 New York normally (DST-aware). UTC-aligned H1 bars mean the first eligible hourly decision is normally 10:00 NY. Close above upper band buys; below lower band sells; any completed outside-band candle qualifies while flat. One position at a time; no DI/ADX/news filter. No same-bar reversal after a midpoint exit.

Main exit: a long exits after a completed H1 close at/below the developing middle band; a short at/above it. This is a closed-bar exit, NOT a continuously moving broker stop. Initial hard stop remains active. Scheduled flattening is 15:58 NY normally and 12:58 NY on published 13:00 half-days, with liquidation one minute before an earlier native broker-session close. This buffer avoids missing final-minute archived quotes and broker trade pauses. Published 2020–2023 holidays and half-days are encoded; the final runs have no cross-session-date holdings. Earlier failed or carried-overnight revisions are diagnostic evidence only, not the final performance comparison. Both controls share the same hard stop/session calendar; fixed 2R removes the middle-band exit, while contrarian flips entry direction and exits favourably back through the middle.

How to interpret the figures

Net-position PF = sum of winning position P&L ÷ absolute sum of losing position P&L, including entry/exit commission and swaps. R = each net position P&L ÷ its original stop cash exposure from the filled entry, then summed; it is not the compounded account return. Overall floating drawdown and native Sharpe come from the tester. Yearly drawdown is CLOSED-BALANCE ONLY, not floating-equity drawdown. Yearly percentages use the continuous run's balance at each year's start, not four independently reset $10,000 accounts. Spread/slippage are embedded in native bid/ask fills. Target risk excludes costs/gaps and is not a guarantee of maximum loss.

Implementation references: MetaQuotes iBands buffers, native ATR, MT5 testing modes. Session calendar: 2020–2022 official calendar, 2022–2023 official calendar. These document the tools/calendar, not evidence for strategy profitability.

See SUMMARY.json for metrics, VERIFICATION.json for independent native-deal/cost/indicator/calendar checks, and native/*/trades.csv for full export. All three final runs share one research-only binary and differ only in the declared entry/exit variant. No production EA, installer, live account or website changed.
